# Meal Log — Vignesh

Daily food tracking. Target: **~2,300–2,400 kcal/day** | 100–120g protein/day | **~25–30g fiber/day**. Canonical source: [[profile]] → Hard Constraints.

> **Fiber rule (for BMI increase):** fiber is for gut/regularity, NOT weight gain — bulky low-cal fiber (raw salad, greens) *fills you up and sabotages the calorie target*. Get fiber only from **calorie-dense sources**: dal/legumes, whole grain (vs white rice/maida), nuts/seeds, dried fruit (dates/figs/raisins), avocado, banana/papaya. These fix constipation AND add gain calories. See [[gastric-remedies]] fiber note.

## Daily Calorie Split (Target Structure)

Front-load the day; dinner is the adjustable patch. As long as breakfast and lunch each land in the 800–1,000 range, dinner patches the rest to ~2,400 without ever exceeding 800.

| Meal | Calorie target | Protein | Fiber | Rule |
|------|---------------|---------|-------|------|
| Breakfast | 800–1,000 | ~30–35g | ~12g | Aim high; **never below 800** |
| Lunch | 800–1,000 | ~45–55g | ~11g | Aim high; **never below 800** |
| Dinner | patch to ~2,400 | ~15–25g | ~5–6g | **Cap 800**; light (~400) when B+L hit 1,000 |
| **Total** | **~2,400** | **~100–115g** | **~28g** | |

> **Self-consistent:** floor B+L (800+800=1,600) + max dinner (800) = 2,400. Top B+L (1,000+1,000=2,000) + light dinner (400) = 2,400. Works at both ends. **Only failure mode: breakfast or lunch below 800** — no legal (≤800) dinner can patch that. Hold the 800 floor on B and L; the rest self-corrects.
> 800 dinner is a *medium* meal, not light — use it as an occasional patch, not routine, or the light-dinner benefit (gastric/sleep) is lost.

### Milkshake — Standing Recipe *(revised 2026-09-02)*

> Referenced throughout this log as **"milkshake (usual recipe, incl. fig)"**. It had no single definition anywhere in the vault despite 20+ entries citing it — this block is that definition.

| Ingredient | Calories | Protein | Fiber |
|------------|----------|---------|-------|
| Milk (~200 ml) | 130 | 6.5g | 0g |
| 1 red banana | 110 | 1.5g | 3g |
| 2 tbsp peanut butter | 190 | 8g | 2g |
| 6 almonds | 42 | 1.8g | 1g |
| 5 cashews | 45 | 1.5g | 0.3g |
| 10 pumpkin seeds | 28 | 1.5g | 0.3g |
| 1 tsp flax seeds | 27 | 1g | 0.7g |
| 5 dry grapes | 15 | 0.2g | 0.2g |
| 1 fig | 30 | 0.4g | 1.4g |
| 3 walnut halves | 39 | 0.7g | 0.5g |
| **½ tbsp sunflower seeds** *(added 2026-09-02)* | 26 | 0.9g | 0.4g |
| **2 dates** *(added 2026-09-02)* | 45 | 0.4g | 1.1g |
| **Total (from 2026-09-02)** | **~736 kcal** | **~24.5g** | **~11.9g** |

**Change log:** ½ tbsp sunflower seeds + 2 dates **added**; **3 walnut halves retained.** Net **+71 kcal / +1.3g protein / +1.5g fiber.** Prior total (in force Aug 17 – Sep 1): **665 / 23.2g / 10.4g**. Before the fig was added (Aug 17): **635 / 22.8g / 9g**.

> ⚠️ **Sequence note:** the change was first instructed as *3 walnut halves → 2 + sunflower + dates* (would have been 723 / 24.3g / 11.7g) and reverted to keep all 3 walnut halves after the omega-3 objection below. **Keeping the walnut costs +13 kcal and +0.2g fiber versus the cut version — a trivial price for keeping the shake's main ALA source intact.**

> ✅ **Objection 2 below was accepted — the walnut cut was reverted the same day and all 3 halves kept. Objection 1 stands and was not addressed.**
>
> **1. The fiber goes the wrong way, at the worst possible moment.** Fiber has now run over the 25–30g target for **nine consecutive weekdays**, and this log's own controlled experiment settled where it comes from: **Aug 31 and Sep 1 had identical lunches and differed only at breakfast — pre-dinner totals 33.8g vs 37.0g, a 3.2g gap exactly equal to the breakfast gap.** Breakfast is the identified lever. **This change moves that lever +1.3g in the wrong direction.** Sep 1's breakfast was 21.8g, the highest in the log; under the new recipe it would have been 23.1g.
>
> **2. ✅ RESOLVED — the walnut cut was an omega-3 downgrade, and it was reverted.** Walnuts are the **only meaningful ALA (omega-3) source in the shake besides 1 tsp of flax.** Sunflower seeds are predominantly **linoleic acid — omega-6.** So the swap cuts ALA by a third *and* adds an omega-6 source, in a shake whose stated purpose includes **HDL 32** ([[lab-reports-summary]]). ✅ **Not one-sided:** sunflower seeds bring vitamin E and **magnesium**, and low magnesium is listed in [[gastric-remedies]] as one of the most common tension-headache/migraine triggers — directly relevant to the current headache thread. **Dates add potassium and iron; both are ~45 kcal of near-pure sugar, which is harmless on a gain plan.**
>
> ⚠️ **One further option, deliberately NOT taken: swapping the 5 dry grapes for the 2 dates** (same category — dried sweet fruit — so nutritionally variety-neutral) would give **721 / 24.3g / 11.7g. That is 15 kcal and 0.2g fiber below the current recipe — below the threshold this log treats as a real difference anywhere else, and not worth acting on.** Recorded so it isn't re-proposed as if it were meaningful.

Beef brain + liver also = very high cholesterol vs your low HDL (32). Fried parotta again (maida → constipation). **Day total ~2,880 kcal — very high (~980 surplus over ~1,900).** → Did you get any SI-joint or other joint pain over Jul 17–19? That's the day to watch after this purine load.

> ✅ **Trip Jul 17–19 backfilled.** See the 4-day purine pattern (Jul 16–19) — organ meats + beer every day; likely cause of the joint pain. Retest uric acid.

> 🔴 **Protocol revised 2026-08-18 — weigh 2–3× per week and judge the weekly average, never a single point.** The Aug 17 → Aug 18 pair (60.60 → 60.10, **−0.50 kg in 24 hours on a 2,498 kcal day**) proves that a single measurement carries roughly **±0.5 kg of noise from water, sodium and gut content** — five times larger than the ~0.1 kg/week signal being tracked. Every week-over-week delta in this table below Aug 3 should be treated as uninterpretable in isolation. **Judge 3–4 week trends only.**

### Why the number swings so much — asked by Vignesh 2026-08-19

**The short answer: the thing being measured is ~0.1 kg/week, and the measurement error is ±0.5 kg/day. The variation is not in the weight gain — it is in the scale.** Nothing has gone wrong with the diet.

**Four things move body weight day to day, none of which are fat or muscle:**

| Source | Size of swing | What drives it here |
|---|---|---|
| **Glycogen + bound water** | **0.4–0.6 kg** | Every gram of stored carbohydrate binds ~3g of water. Rice, idiyappam and banana intake swing daily; the water follows. |
| **Sodium** | **0.3–0.8 kg** | Restaurant food, Pringles, biryani, alcohol. The Aug 14–16 weekend is the clearest example in this table — retention peaked Aug 17 at 60.60 and cleared over 48 hrs to 59.85. |
| **Gut content** | **0.3–1.0 kg** | Undigested food + water in transit. Fiber intake here has ranged **4.8g to 39.3g** in a single fortnight; that alone moves the scale by several hundred grams. |
| **Hydration** | **0.2–0.5 kg** | Sweat, EFX sessions, fluid timing against the 6:30 PM cutoff. |

**Stack those and ±0.5–1.0 kg of daily noise is normal and expected.** Against a genuine gain of **~0.35 kg per week**, a single weigh-in cannot resolve the signal — it is like trying to read a whisper next to a running fan.

**The arithmetic proof, from this log's own numbers:** Aug 17 → Aug 19 shows −0.75 kg. Actual intake across those days was ~2,498 and ~2,425 kcal against a ~2,050 maintenance. **Losing 0.75 kg of real tissue in 48 hours would require burning roughly 5,350 kcal/day.** That did not happen. Therefore the 0.75 kg was never tissue.

**What to do instead — three rules:**
1. **Weigh 3 mornings a week** (e.g. Mon/Wed/Fri), same conditions: empty stomach, post-toilet, minimal clothing.
2. **Average the three. Record only the average as the week's weight.**
3. **Compare averages to averages, never a single day to a single day.** Two averages that differ by less than ~0.3 kg are the same number.

⚠️ **The real risk is behavioural, not physiological: reacting to noise.** A scare from a low reading invites over-eating or discouragement; a high reading invites complacency or an unnecessary trim. **Both have already happened in this log** — the Aug 10 "deceleration" and the Aug 17 "1.85 kg/month spike" were each acted on in the notes and each had to be retracted. **The correct response to any single weigh-in is to change nothing.**

✅ **The trend that survives all of this: Jul 6 → Aug 19, 58.0 → 60.18 kg (3-day avg) = +2.18 kg in 44 days ≈ 1.5 kg/month.** That is on plan. **The gain is not stalling — it is being measured too finely.**


### Waist Tracking (added 2026-08-31)

> 🔴 **Baseline not yet taken — record it at the next weigh-in.** Weight says *how much*; **waist says what kind.** With HDL 32, an inflamed spine and an unresolved airway question, what kind is the whole question — and the baseline is only obtainable *before* the +14 kg to 75 kg, not after. Full thresholds and rationale in [[bmi-increase-plan]].

**How:** at the navel, standing relaxed, **after a normal exhale** — not sucked in, not pushed out. Same morning as the weigh-in, 3×/week.

**Thresholds (Indian male):** ✅ **< 85 cm** gaining the right tissue · ⚠️ **85–89 cm** slow the rate, check training progression · 🔴 **≥ 90 cm** abdominal obesity — the surplus has become fat.

**Budget to 75 kg: no more than ~+4.5 cm total** (rule of thumb: under ~1 cm of waist per 3 kg of bodyweight). Anything faster means the composition is wrong regardless of the scale.

✅ **Composition is better** — oats, flax, chia, and walnuts are four of the best HDL/omega-3 items on the [[bmi-increase-plan]] calorie-dense fiber table, all in one bowl. Directly relevant at HDL 32.

⚠️ **But the swap loses ~70 kcal and ~7g protein vs the milkshake it replaced** (635/22.8g → 565/15.75g). Protein is the real gap — the shake's milk was carrying it. **Fix: make the oats with ~200ml full-fat milk in addition to the yogurt** (+~130 kcal, +~6.5g protein). [[bmi-increase-plan]] already specifies "oats in full-fat milk, 40g ≈ 300 kcal" — this bowl used 100ml yogurt (60 kcal) as its only liquid, which is why it under-delivers.

> ✅ **Trim question re-opened and answered 2026-08-17: still do not trim.** The Aug 17 weigh-in puts the two-week trend at ~1.85 kg/month, back above the 1.2 kg/month trigger that originally called for a ~150 kcal/day cut. **It should still not be applied, for a reason that did not exist on Aug 3:** actual logged intake over Aug 11–16 averaged **~2,338/day — already at the bottom of the 2,300–2,400 band**, and the weekend fell to ~2,280 and ~1,950 with two skipped dinners. **Trimming a target that is being undershot is backwards.** The gain is coming from a maintenance level lower than modeled, not from overeating. Revisit only if intake is reliably hitting 2,350+ *and* the rate is still above 1.2 kg/month at the Aug 24 weigh-in.
>
> 🔴 **The Aug 3 "~150 kcal/day trim" recommendation is now STALE — do not apply it.** It was written when the gain rate was ~1.9 kg/month (too fast). **The 2026-08-10 weigh-in shows the rate collapsed to +0.05 kg for the week.** Cutting calories against a flatlining trend is backwards. Until the trend re-establishes, the priority is holding **2,300–2,500 kcal and 100–120g protein** — not trimming. Any earlier note in this log framing a calorie reduction as a win is superseded by this.

⚠️ **Tea taken BEFORE breakfast — breaks a documented hard rule.** [[profile]] constraint: *"Caffeine: coffee only after food, never on empty stomach (cortisol spike)."* [[anxiety-stress]] states it more strongly — *"Coffee after food, not before — no exceptions. Caffeine on empty stomach directly triggers cortisol release before the day even starts."* Tea is lower-caffeine than coffee but the mechanism is the same, and this lands on day 1 of the new 5:30 AM wake, when the cortisol awakening response is already elevated. **Move tea to after the meal.**

🔴 **Reported same morning: "stomach fully, dumped feeling" despite the lower calorie count.** This is the exact failure mode [[bmi-increase-plan]] names: *"High-volume low-cal fiber fills the stomach and sabotages the calorie target — the opposite of what an underweight person needs."* Three mechanisms stacked:
> 1. **Soluble fiber gels.** Oat beta-glucan, chia, and flax all absorb many times their weight in water — chia roughly 10×. 40g oats + 1 tsp chia + 1 tsp flax expands into far more stomach volume than 565 kcal would suggest.
> 2. **The ramp was skipped.** Fiber went from ~4.8g (Aug 9) to **20.7g in a single breakfast** — a ~4× jump. [[gastric-remedies]] says explicitly: *"Ramp legumes/chia slowly and hydrate in daytime — you're gastric-prone."*
> 3. **Fat + cold slow gastric emptying.** Nuts, PB, and ghee omelette are fat-dense; overnight oats with yogurt are eaten cold. Both delay emptying, extending the fullness.
>
> **Fix — keep the composition, cut the single-sitting volume:** split the bowl (half at breakfast, half mid-morning ~10:30 as the [[bmi-increase-plan]] snack slot already allows); drop chia and flax to ½ tsp each and ramp back over 2 weeks; add ~200ml full-fat milk (calories without proportional bulk). **Do not add more fiber at lunch today.**

**Note:** Breakfast comparison vs Aug 10 (1,091 → 980, −111 kcal): omelette had no cheese (−60), sweet corn dropped (−96), half apple absorbed into the bowl (−45), milkshake→oats (−70), offset by added cashew/pista/PB (+160). **The milkshake swap is not the main driver of the drop** — the missing cheese and sweet corn account for more of it.

**Notes — 2026-08-11 final:**

✅ **Calories and protein both in band** — ~2,445 kcal (near the top, correct given the flatlined gain rate) and ~108.5g protein. Lunch and dinner carried ~73g protein after the light breakfast, as needed.

✅ **Purine run broken** — first day since Aug 7 with no beef, mutton, organ meat, or shellfish. The uric-acid retest recommendation from Aug 10 still stands regardless.

✅ **Dinner respected the rule** — paneer fried rice, no chicken/fish, low-fiber (the correct call given fiber was already over target).

🔴 **Fiber ~37.9g — 8–13g over target, and symptomatic.** Bloating/fullness reported after the 20.7g single-sitting breakfast bolus. See the new [[gastric-remedies]] "Fiber Ramp Rate" section — this day is the source case for it.

⚠️ **Tea taken before breakfast** — breaks the documented no-caffeine-on-empty-stomach rule, on day 1 of the 5:30 AM wake.

⚠️ **Sleep disturbed 2 nights running** (vivid/disturbing dreams) — reported same day. Candidate causes logged in conversation: REM rebound from the new 7.5h window, the unconfirmed-alcohol mojito (Aug 10), and possible skipped dinners Aug 8/Aug 10. **Two open questions never answered: was the mojito alcoholic, and was dinner eaten on Aug 8 and Aug 10?**

### Lunch notes

⚠️ **Italian B.M.T. = salami + pepperoni + ham — cured/fermented meat is among the highest-histamine foods there is.** [[profile]] hard constraints bar high-histamine/fermented foods on a sinus flare, with active right maxillary sinusitis and Allegra 120mg running. The histamine flag in this log has been almost entirely about **chilli**; cured meat is the other half of the same pathway and has never been called out before. Watch tonight for the usual signature — body heat, nasal fluid, head sloshing. Also a heavy sodium/nitrate load against HDL 32.

✅ **Protein efficiency is genuinely good** — 41g for 604 kcal is essentially the Buddha bowl's ratio (45g / 595 kcal). As a bowl substitute on the macros alone, it holds up; the problem is the cured-meat filling, not the format.

🔴 **Fiber blown past the target at lunch, not dinner.** The wrap's spinach tortilla brought 11.5g — *more* than the Buddha bowl's 10g, the opposite of what the breakfast note called for. Day stands at **~36g before dinner exists**, already over the 25–30 band. **Dinner must be zero-fiber** — eggs/paneer only, no sambar, no dal, no chutney, no banana. This will still close as the 5th consecutive day over target.

**Dinner needs to land ~350–450 kcal and ~15–25g protein** to bring the day into the 2,300–2,400 band at 100–120g protein. 3 eggs (~18g) does it. Well under the 800 cap, and no chicken/fish per the rule.

**Note:** 604 kcal / 41g / 11.5g are Subway's published figures, not estimates — the most reliable lunch entry in the log. 11.5g read as **fiber** (spinach tortilla is a high-fiber wrap, matches the log's column order) — correct if it was fat.

### Dinner notes (backfilled 2026-08-17)

🔴 **120ml scotch ≈ 4.8 units — the largest single-session alcohol dose in this log.** It exceeds the Aug 1 session (~4.5 units: 650ml beer + 60ml vodka), which this log already called *"meaningful, not incidental."* Urate excretion is blocked for ~24h, so the block ran through most of Aug 15. **The uric acid retest flagged "important, not optional" in mid-July is now ~4 weeks past that call and still outstanding.**

✅ **Scotch over beer is the correct swap on purines** — spirits are essentially purine-free, while beer's brewer's yeast is high-purine and is what every prior alcohol flag in this log (Jul 16–19, Aug 1) was attached to. One of the two urate mechanisms removed; the excretion block remains and scales with dose.

✅ **Cucumber, carrot and eggs match the low-histamine, zero-purine snack pattern** — fresh veg and eggs, nothing cured, fried, or shellfish. This is a genuine departure from the log's usual bar-food default (chicken 65, beef fry, prawn).

🔴 **Tandoori chicken at dinner — rule break, 6th recorded** (Jul 10, 15, 24, 25, Aug 1, Aug 14). [[profile]] bars chicken and fish at dinner. Compounding it: tandoori carries marinade-level chilli (a confirmed histamine trigger, on Allegra 120mg) **alongside alcohol, itself a histamine liberator** — the same stack flagged on Aug 1. The [[gastric-remedies]] fat + alcohol + late-meal chain also predicts a next-morning gastric headache.

⚠️ **Alcohol was ~11% of the day's calories** (270 of 2,529) — lower share than Aug 1's ~18%, but zero protein and zero fiber, and it lands on the sleep question below.

⚠️ **Sleep.** Aug 11–13 logged 3+ nights of disturbed sleep, and [[anxiety-stress]] has an open medication-review trigger on sleep onset since Melatonin stopped. Alcohol at this dose fragments the second half of the night and suppresses deep sleep — the window where most testosterone production happens ([[healthspan-80]] Pillar 7, testosterone still unmeasured).

**Day close:** ~2,529 kcal (~130 over the 2,300–2,400 band), protein 116.5g ✅ in band, dinner 575 kcal ✅ well under the 800 cap. **Fiber ~37.9g — 5th consecutive day over target**, as predicted at lunch; the day was lost on fiber at lunch, not dinner (cucumber + carrot added only 1.9g).

**Open questions:** (1) **What time did the session end?** [[profile]] bars all food *and liquid* after 6:30 PM — a 120ml scotch is unlikely to have closed by then, but it's not recorded. (2) **Aug 15 and Aug 16 are entirely unlogged.**

Alcohol blocks urate excretion for ~24h, so this purine load hit a system that had not cleared. **This is a verbatim repeat of the Aug 1–2 mechanism** the log already spelled out: *"alcohol blocks urate excretion for ~24h, so today's purine load lands on a system that hasn't cleared yesterday's."* With uric acid 5.3, seronegative inflammatory arthritis and SI-joint history, and the **retest now ~4 weeks past being called "important, not optional,"** this is the pattern that preceded joint pain in July. **Watch the SI joint through Aug 16–18.**

✅ **Tender coconut was the right instinct on the one axis that mattered this weekend — hydration and urate clearance.** Increased fluid intake is one of the few genuinely effective levers on renal urate excretion, and this landed on the same day as a prawn + squid purine load, one day after 4.8 units of alcohol. Also zero purine, zero histamine, gut-friendly, potassium-rich. **It does not offset the shellfish load** — it helps at the margin — but of the snacks available it is the one that works *with* the problem instead of against it.

⚠️ **Same choice works against the calorie target, though.** ~180 kcal for two full coconuts' worth of malai is high volume for very little energy — precisely the pattern this file's own fiber rule warns about: *"bulky low-cal fiber fills you up and sabotages the calorie target."* At BMI 17.7 with the gain rate flatlined, filling up on tender coconut is a real cost. Mature coconut flesh would have delivered ~4× the calories in the same eating time.

✅ **Breakfast ~880 kcal — clears the 800 floor** once the idli/poori/sambar are counted. The full tiffin plate (pongal + vadai + idli + poori + sambar + omelette) is what carried it; the half pongal alone would have failed the floor.

✅ **The 5-day fiber overshoot streak is broken** — 37.9 → 15.3g. Still under the 25–30 target, but this is a controlled correction rather than the collapse it looked like before the snacks were counted (the pre-snack read was 7.1g).

🔴 **~2,280 kcal — just under the 2,300 floor** if no dinner was eaten. Not a large miss, but it lands on a **flatlined gain rate** (+0.05 kg last week), and the shortfall is entirely a composition problem: a seafood lunch and a tender-coconut snack are both low-energy-density choices.

⚠️ **Protein ~167g — far over the 120 ceiling**, most of it from one seafood-heavy meal (~128g at lunch alone). The day overshot protein by ~47g while finishing under the calorie floor — protein was never the gap.

⚠️ **Poori is deep-fried maida** — refined flour, no fiber, fried oil against HDL 32. Calorie-useful at BMI 17.7, which is why it isn't a red flag, but it's the same maida-at-a-low-fiber-meal pattern flagged on Jul 15 and Jul 23.

**Estimate note:** 2 tender coconuts' malai taken as ~275g at ~65 kcal/100g. Tender coconut ranges widely — very young jelly-like malai is ~40 kcal/100g (~110 kcal total), semi-mature sliceable malai closer to ~100 kcal/100g (~275 kcal total). "Slices" implies the firmer semi-mature form, hence the mid-high estimate. Range on the day: ~2,210–2,375 kcal.

>
> **Aug 14** — 120ml scotch ≈ 4.8 units, the largest session in the log; alcohol blocks urate excretion for ~24h
> **Aug 15** — prawn + squid (shellfish, both on the *"avoid in excess"* list), landing inside that block

The Daily Calorie Split table names a sub-800 breakfast as the *one* failure mode nothing downstream can patch, and that is exactly how the day ended ~435–535 under band.

🔴 **Coffee almost certainly on an empty stomach.** [[profile]] rule: *coffee only after food, never on empty stomach (cortisol spike).* Tender coconut water is not food. **Confirm** — but as recorded this is a rule break, and it lands during the ongoing disturbed-sleep/cortisol stretch flagged Aug 11–13.

🔴 **The clearest single lesson in this entry — the junk almost exactly equals the shortfall.** Half coconut cake (180) + 4 biscuits (100) + 8 Pringles (85) = **~365 kcal of refined sugar, refined starch and fried oil**, carrying ~4g protein and ~1.6g fiber between them. The day finished **~400 short**. Swapping that ~365 kcal for the milkshake or a handful of nuts would have closed nearly the whole gap *and* delivered protein, magnesium and omega-3. The calories were available; they were spent on nothing. **Three separate ultra-processed snack items in one afternoon is a first for this log.**

- **"Half coconut cake"** read as half a slice/piece of bakery-style coconut cake (~180 kcal). If it was half a whole cake, or a coconut burfi/sweet, this is materially different — the log's one established coconut sweet is 90 kcal.

The uric acid retest called *"important, not optional"* in mid-July is now **~4–5 weeks overdue**, and there is no longer a plausible reading in which that is a low-priority item. **Book it today.** Flag the pattern to Dr. Rajapandian ([[profile]] — seronegative inflammatory arthritis, SI-joint history, uric acid 5.3).

A modest overshoot today recovers it. **Do not repeat the token-dinner pattern to protect a single day's number.**
>
> **Dinner shape: carb + fat, low protein, low fiber, no chicken/fish.** Protein is 26g over ceiling and fiber is at the top of its useful range, so the useful calories are ghee/oil and starch — ghee dosa, ghee rice with curd, or the paneer fried rice used on Aug 11 (noted then as "the correct call given fiber was already over target"). ✅✅ **FIBER LANDED IN RANGE — ~27.9g, first time since Aug 9.** Nine days of either massive overshoot (36–42g, Aug 10–14) or collapse (4.8–15.3g, Aug 15–16), and this is the first clean landing. **The half red banana at lunch is what did it** — the Aug 12–13 structural fix, finally applied.

Protein above ~1.6–2.0 g/kg (≈100–120g here) adds nothing for muscle gain and displaces the calorie-dense choices that would.

⚠️ **Sambar before bed again** — dal ferments overnight while horizontal ([[gastric-remedies]] next-morning-headache chain). Small cup, which is the right size, but this is the 4th+ occurrence flagged (Aug 12, 13, 17).

✅ **Week-level read: calories are not actually the problem the weekend suggested.** Aug 11–17 averaged **~2,361 kcal/day — dead centre of the 2,300–2,400 band.** The failure was **variance, not the mean**: 1,950 to 2,529 across seven days, with two skipped dinners. Consistency is the lever, not total intake — and it lines up with the Aug 17 decision not to trim.

**Recipe change — 1 dried fig added to the standard milkshake, effective today.** Standard values move to **~665 kcal / 23.2g protein / 10.4g fiber**; see the Micronutrient section for the full rationale and the two caveats (it does nothing for calories, and it nudges weekday fiber further over target). **Notes — 2026-08-18 (day closed; dinner backfilled 2026-08-19):**

🔴🔴 **EGG WHITES ARE THE WRONG MOVE — switch back to whole eggs.** This is the first egg-white entry in the log, and it appears the day after the abdominal-fat conversation. **It is exactly the "cut the fat" instinct that was advised against.** What it costs:
>
> | | 4 egg whites | 4 whole eggs |
> |---|---|---|
> | Calories | ~68 | ~280 |
> | Protein | 14.4g | 24g |
> | Vitamin D | **0** | ~1.6 µg |
> | Choline, B12, lutein | **0** | all in the yolk |
>
> **~210 kcal thrown away at BMI 17.9, for no benefit.** Protein was already ~45g over ceiling yesterday, so *reducing* protein is correct — but the right way is **fewer whole eggs, not more whites.** 2 whole eggs give 12g protein and 140 kcal; 4 whites give 14.4g and 68 kcal. Same protein, half the calories, none of the micronutrients.
>
> 🔴 **The vitamin D point is the serious one.** [[lab-reports-summary]] records Vit D 29.95 = **insufficient**, last measured **Jul 2024 — two years stale**, and it is the *only* confirmed deficiency on record. **Egg yolk is one of very few dietary vitamin D sources available.** Discarding yolks while the D3 supplement adherence is still unrecorded (see Micronutrient table) removes the one food source without replacing it. Yolk also carries **lutein and zeaxanthin**, directly relevant to the ongoing screen/eye-strain problem in [[eye-strain-remedies]].

⚠️ **Gastric headache this morning, attributed by Vignesh to a partially unripe red banana (Aug 17).** Candidate ranking **revised 2026-08-18 after honey was disclosed** — **two brand-new foods were introduced on Aug 17, which is why the near-daily banana is the weakest explanation, not the strongest:**
>
> | # | Candidate | Case |
> |---|-----------|------|
> | **1** | 🔴 **Honey, 1 tbsp at ~9:30 PM** | **First-ever exposure, on the exact night before the symptom, and eaten closest to going horizontal.** ~17g sugar, ~40% **fructose** — a FODMAP that ferments in the colon. Also **breaks the 6:30 PM no-food-or-liquid rule by ~3 hours**, which exists specifically to prevent this. |
> | **2** | **Sambar before bed** | Repeat offender, flagged Aug 12, 13, 17 — *"dal ferments overnight while horizontal."* Occurred the same night. |
> | **3** | 🔴 **Dried fig** | **First-ever exposure, Aug 17 breakfast.** High in fructans and sorbitol. |
> | **4** | **Duck** | Half plate biryani + 8 pieces; fatty and slow-emptying — the Aug 1 next-morning chain. |
> | **5** | **Unripe red banana** | Mechanism real (resistant starch ferments), but **red banana has been eaten near-daily for six weeks without this symptom.** Partial unripeness is a small perturbation against four larger ones. |
>
> **Background contributor:** ~165.6g protein on Aug 17, ~45g over ceiling — high protein loads increase colonic fermentation independently of any single food.
>
> **Clean test order (change one variable at a time, in evidence order): drop the honey first, then sambar, then the fig.**

🔴 **Honey before sleep — stop it, and note that it targets the wrong problem.** Started Aug 17 for sleep. Three separate objections:
>
> 1. **It breaks a hard constraint** — [[profile]]: *no food or liquid of any kind after 6:30 PM (gastric + sleep protection).* Sleep is 10 PM, so 9:30 PM is ~3 hours past the cutoff. The rule exists to prevent exactly the overnight-fermentation → morning-headache chain that then occurred.
> 2. **The proposed mechanism addresses the wrong symptom.** The honey-before-bed claim (liver glycogen → prevents a nocturnal cortisol/adrenaline surge) is aimed at **night-waking**. The documented problem here is **sleep *onset* latency** ([[anxiety-stress]], Melatonin stopped ~Jan 2026). Honey does not target onset. Evidence for honey and adult sleep is thin regardless — the solid honey RCTs are for nocturnal cough in children, not sleep.
> 3. **~17g of sugar 30 minutes before sleep can cause the awakening it claims to prevent** — glucose spike followed by a reactive dip.
>
> **Better onset levers already in the file, none of which break the rule:** extend the evening meditation (see [[anxiety-stress]] upgrade table), switch it to a lying-down body scan / NSDR, warm shower, brain dump, and **CBT-I** — the only sleep intervention with first-line evidence, worth raising at the overdue psychiatry appointment.

🔴 **The fig test is already contaminated — it is in today's milkshake too.** Two consecutive days of exposure. **Cleanest sequence from here: drop sambar/dal at dinner tonight (change nothing else). If the headache recurs tomorrow, drop the fig next.** Eat ripe bananas regardless — zero cost — but do not remove red banana as a food; it is ~110 kcal, potassium, 3g fiber, and it is what fixed the Aug 17 fiber landing.

⚠️ **This is a high-fermentation breakfast to eat on a gassy morning.** Apple (sorbitol + excess fructose), litchi (high fructose), fig (fructans), padam pisin (soluble gum), plus the milkshake's PB and seeds. Nutritionally excellent, poorly timed — **on a gastric-headache morning, favour low-FODMAP fruit** (ripe banana, papaya, melon, berries) over apple and litchi.

✅ **Breakfast ~980 kcal — top of the 800–1,000 target**, and it would have been ~1,190 with whole eggs.

✅ **Protein ~41.2g — appropriately moderate**, correcting yesterday's overshoot. Keep the day near ~120g total.

⚠️ **Protein ~33.2g is light for breakfast** (Aug 18–20 ran 41–43g). A normal Buddha-bowl lunch (45g) plus a light dinner still clears the 100g floor comfortably, so this is not a problem, just a note for context.

✅ **TEST READOUT — no headache on waking Aug 21** (reported 2026-08-21), following the Aug 20 chicken-gravy/no-sambar/no-honey dinner (rule break). **Third consecutive clean morning** (Aug 19 confounded by Allegra, Aug 20 clean, Aug 21 clean) with sambar and honey both absent across all three nights. **The evidence is accumulating in one direction: no headache across three different dinners that share only "no honey, no sambar" in common** — chicken gravy, idli/eggs, and idli/eggs again. This is now a reasonable basis to treat **sambar and honey as the two live suspects, with the food elimination working as intended**, even though Aug 19 alone can't be cited due to the Allegra confound.

🔴 **But a new, more important problem replaced the headache: disturbed sleep from dreams, including wet dreams, reported the same morning — "feel like didn't get enough sleep."** This does not roll back the gastric-headache finding above; it is a separate thread that now needs its own entry. See [[anxiety-stress]] — filed there together with the standing morning-sleepiness investigation, since both concern sleep quality rather than food.

✅✅ **Fiber landed inside target — first time since Aug 17.** Day at ~23.9g with dinner still ahead; a fiber-light dinner (eggs + rice/idiyappam + curd, no sambar/dal) keeps this in the 25–30g band for the first clean close in five days. **This is the payoff of fixing breakfast rather than lunch** — exactly the structural point made in yesterday's and today's morning notes.

⚠️ **Subway "rice bowl" is a new dish with no established rate — valued at the numbers Vignesh reported (600 kcal / 23g protein) rather than a log estimate, since Subway packaging/app gives exact figures. Fiber estimated at ~8g** (rice + vegetables + likely beans/legume topping) — **flag if the app or receipt gives an exact fiber figure, since this is the least certain number in today's log.**

✅ **Corrected to 2 tbsp PB — the usual full serving.** Protein ~66.7g at the lunch mark, before the pork dinner below.

### Dinner (backfilled 2026-08-24) — 6 pieces pork gravy + 2 idli

🔴 **First-ever pork entry in this log.** Estimated at the established fatty-meat-piece rate (~70 kcal / 8g protein per piece, in line with mutton/goose) — **420 kcal / 48g protein / ~0g fiber for 6 pieces. Flag if the pieces were smaller** (mutton-piece range in this log has run 50–80 kcal each depending on size).

🔴 **Purine chain resumes after a 1-day break.** Aug 20 (chicken) was the first purine-light dinner in the run; **pork is moderate-to-high purine**, well above poultry though below organ meat/shellfish/game birds. Combined with the still-overdue uric acid retest (now ~5–6 weeks past the point it was called "important, not optional"), this re-opens rather than closes that watch item.

⚠️ **Fat load close to sleep, same mechanism flagged for duck** — gravy-cooked pork is fat-dense and slows gastric emptying. Not a fiber problem (0g), but relevant to the same overnight-symptom investigation for a different reason: a heavy, slow-emptying dinner can produce reflux/fullness discomfort independent of fermentable fiber.

🔴 **Fourth different dinner shape in the honey/sambar elimination sequence, and none of the last three has repeated the last.** Aug 19 idli+sambar+eggs (Allegra confound) → Aug 20 idli+chicken gravy (rule break) → Aug 21 idli+pork (new food, no sambar again). **Sambar and honey remain absent for three straight nights, which keeps strengthening them as the suspects**, but each night has also introduced a new, untested food (chicken gravy, then pork) — if a headache appears on Aug 22, it will be exactly as hard to attribute as Aug 20's was.

🔴 **Day closed at ~2,445 kcal — over target, and ~118.7g protein — near the top of the 100–120g band**, a swing from this morning's under-100g trajectory straight past the ceiling. The pork alone (48g) is more than double what a "light dinner" was supposed to contribute (~15–20g). **Fiber closed at 26.9g — inside the 25–30g target**, the first clean landing since Aug 17, and it held despite the large dinner because pork itself carries none.

✅ **Rule-compliant on the letter of the constraint** — [[profile]] restricts chicken and fish at dinner specifically; pork isn't on that list. **Worth deciding explicitly whether pork should be added to it** — the rule exists for spice/histamine-trigger and purine-load reasons that apply to pork too, just not tested here before.

🔴 **Ice cream added after the pork+idli dinner — breaks the "nothing after 6:30 PM" rule almost by definition,** since dessert follows a meal that already runs late most nights. Dairy fat + sugar on top of an already fat-heavy pork dinner **compounds the slow-gastric-emptying concern above rather than adding a new one** — same mechanism, larger dose. **Total day now closes at ~2,595 kcal, ~130 over the 2,300–2,400 target, and ~121.2g protein — just over the 120g ceiling.** Fiber holds at 26.9g since ice cream carries none. **This is now the heaviest and latest-running dinner in the honey/sambar elimination sequence** — if Aug 22 brings a headache, the ice cream/fat load is at least as plausible a cause as anything upstream, and should be named as a candidate alongside pork.

**Note:** Values at the established rates — half ven pongal 160/5g/1g and 1 ulundhu vadai 140/4g/1.5g (both Aug 15). Milkshake at the post-fig standard.

This is a genuine once-a-year festival meal (rice + 11 traditional dishes on a banana leaf: sambar, rasam, avial, thoran, olan, pachadi, erissery, pickle, papadam, banana chips, payasam, pulissery), not a habitual pattern — **treat today like the occasional social/festival days already in this log (e.g. Aug 1–2), not as a diet failure to correct.** ⚠️ **This is still a first-time dish with no established rate and real estimate uncertainty** — the corrected breakdown assumes a typical moderate serving of each remaining item and could realistically be off by ±300 kcal in either direction.

✅ **Protein is fine at 78.2g** — a modest, mostly-vegetarian Sadhya still clears close to four-fifths of the 100g floor.

🔴 **Given ~2,829 kcal is already ~430–530 over the top of the 2,300–2,400 band, and fiber at 36.1g is ~6–11g over the 25–30g target, tonight's dinner should stay light** — the overshoot is real but far smaller than first estimated, so a normal light dinner (not a skip) is reasonable. Keep it protein-focused (e.g. 2 eggs) rather than another carb-heavy dish to avoid adding further to fiber.

✅ **Dinner actually followed the recommendation — 1 dosa + 2 eggs (260 kcal, 1g fiber), the light protein-focused meal flagged above, not another carb-heavy Sadhya-style dish.** Rule-compliant too (no chicken/fish). **Total (day closed): ~3,089 kcal / ~93.2g protein / ~37.1g fiber** — calories land ~690–790 over the 2,300–2,400 band and fiber ~7–12g over the 25–30g target, both driven entirely by the Sadhya lunch (~1,784 kcal alone), not by breakfast or dinner. Protein at 93.2g sits just under the 100g floor — the one gap on an otherwise disciplined day. Consistent with treating this as a one-off festival day: the excess is concentrated in a single identifiable meal, not spread across the day's structure.

It isn't a fiber-quantity problem, it's a variance problem**, exactly as the Aug 17 note concluded for calories: *"the failure was variance, not the mean."* The 6-day average lands near target while not one individual day is anywhere near it. ✅ **The fix is already written down and still not applied: keep the milkshake on Saturday and Sunday.** It would have added ~10g fiber, ~665 kcal of gain calories, ~45% of the magnesium RDA and essentially all the day's omega-3 — one item covering four gaps at once.

Aug 26's Sadhya (~3,089) stands as the one-off it was originally logged as.

⚠️ **10 pieces of chicken 65 is the largest single spicy/histamine load recorded.** The previous flag was Aug 1 at 8 pieces — and that was *across two meals*. ⚠️ **No weigh-in reported.** If one is taken Aug 30, expect it inflated: biryani, fried chicken and restaurant-level sodium is the same signature that produced the +0.65 kg Aug 27 reading after the Sadhya. **Do not read it as tissue.**

✅✅ **Day at ~24g fiber with dinner still to come — the best weekend fiber figure in the log by a wide margin, and it closes the constipation watch opened yesterday.** The two structural weekend fixes prescribed since Aug 17 — keep the milkshake, and put vegetables in the weekend lunch — both happened on the same day for the first time.

⚠️ **The calorie headroom for dinner is tight: ~205–305 kcal to land inside the 2,300–2,400 band.** That is well under half a normal dinner here (idiyappam + coconut milk + 2 eggs runs ~435).

🔴 **The calorie ceiling and the protein floor are close to conflicting, and the lemon sodas are why.** Protein is 77.2g and needs **~22.8g more** to clear the 100g floor; calories allow **≤305 kcal**. That demands better than ~13 kcal per gram of protein, which rules out almost everything except eggs and curd. **The 2 lemon sodas were 180 kcal for zero protein — the exact room the dinner now lacks.** This is the concrete cost of the empty-sugar pattern flagged yesterday, arriving one day later as a real constraint rather than a general complaint.

✅ **Dinner that resolves it cleanly: 4 boiled eggs (~280 kcal, ~24g protein)** → closes the day at **~2,375 kcal and ~101.2g protein, both inside band.** ⚠️ **The rasam is what made this work** — before it, the protein gap needed eggs *plus* curd and the arithmetic didn't fit under the ceiling. **Buttermilk instead of curd if anything is added** (35 kcal vs ~120).

⚠️ **Fiber will close near 24g, just under the 25–30g target — accept that.** Eggs add none, and chasing the last gram with fruit costs ~70–95 kcal that the protein floor needs more. **After yesterday's 4.5g, a 24g weekend day is not a miss worth correcting.**

🔴 **6 glasses of lemon soda across two days.** Carbonated drinks are on the [[gastric-remedies]] avoid-list, and the Jul 12 note already said "keep occasional."

**Estimate note — three new dishes with no established rates in the log, all first appearances:**
- **Snake gourd kootu ~180/8g/5g** — snake gourd is very low-calorie (~20 kcal/100g); nearly all the calories and protein come from the toor/moong dal and coconut, and the dal is what makes this the day's best fiber-per-calorie item.
- **Fried ladies finger ~200/2g/3.5g** — okra itself is ~33 kcal/100g with ~3.2g fiber; **~170 of the 200 kcal here is absorbed frying oil.** The fiber is real, the calorie density is not the vegetable's fault. If it was shallow-fried rather than deep-fried, subtract ~60–80 kcal.
- **Beans with egg poriyal ~180/8g/3.5g** — assumes ~1 egg, ~100g beans, coconut and a teaspoon of oil. **Flag if more than one egg went in**, which would add ~70 kcal / 6g protein and ease the dinner protein squeeze.

**Dinner notes — 2026-08-30 (day closed at ~2,895 kcal / ~100.2g protein / ~33.6g fiber):**

🔴🔴 **Dinner came in at ~800 kcal against the ~205–305 the day had room for — roughly 3× over — and the day closes ~495–595 above the 2,300–2,400 band.** This is the second-highest calorie day since Aug 26's Sadhya. ⚠️ **The cause is structural and easy to name: dinner repeated lunch.** Rice, rasam, ladies finger and beans poriyal were all eaten twice, so the evening meal was effectively a second lunch rather than the light protein close the day needed.

🔴 **The protein target was hit, but bought at ~3× the price.** ~23g of protein for ~800 kcal is **~35 kcal per gram**. The 4 boiled eggs recommended at lunchtime were ~24g for ~280 kcal — **~11.7 kcal per gram.** Same protein, ~520 fewer calories. ✅ **Protein closed at 100.2g and cleared the floor**, which is the good news; the overshoot is entirely a *source* problem, not an amount problem.

⚠️ **Note also that dinner sat exactly at the 800 kcal cap, so the cap didn't catch this.** The binding constraint today was the day total, not the meal size — worth remembering, because the cap can be respected while the band is missed by 500.

✅ **Dinner was rule-compliant** — vegetarian plus one boiled egg, no chicken or fish. ✅ **And it was the right *shape* of food**; the problem was that it duplicated a meal already eaten, not that any single item was wrong.

🔴 **Fiber closed at 33.6g — over the 25–30g target, after yesterday closed at an all-time-low 4.5g.** ⚠️ **That is a ~7.5× jump in 24 hours, and this log has an explicit precedent against it**: the Aug 11 note flagged a ~4× fiber jump (4.8g → 20.7g) with the [[gastric-remedies]] rule *"ramp legumes/chia slowly and hydrate in daytime — you're gastric-prone."* Today's jump is nearly twice as steep, and it came via dal, beans and okra twice over. **Watch for bloating or gastric discomfort tonight and tomorrow morning.**

🔴 **The weekend restates the variance finding rather than resolving it.** Aug 29 · Aug 30 = 4.5g and 33.6g fiber; 2,520 and 2,895 kcal. **The two-day fiber average is ~19g, which is under target while neither day was anywhere near it** — the same pattern the weekday stretch showed in reverse. ✅ **Real progress worth keeping: the milkshake and a vegetable-heavy weekend lunch both happened, and those are the two fixes that were prescribed.** 🔴 **The remaining gap is the one this log keeps finding at every scale — consistency, not composition.**

⚠️ **Two fried items on the same day (ladies finger at lunch and again at dinner) plus banana chips.** Around ~340 kcal of the day is absorbed frying oil, against HDL 32. Banana chips are also the day's third empty-calorie item after the 2 lemon sodas — ~290 kcal combined for ~1g protein.

✅ **The lemon-soda point holds and is now measurable across two days.** 6 glasses Aug 29–30 = ~540 kcal for zero protein, zero fiber, zero micronutrients — more than a full dinner's worth of the band, spent on nothing.

**Estimate note:** ladies finger and beans poriyal valued at the same rates set at lunch today (200/2g/3.5g and 180/8g/3.5g) — **if the evening servings were smaller leftovers, subtract up to ~150 kcal.** Banana chips taken at the established 10-piece rate of 110/1g/1g (Jul 23); "some" is imprecise, and a larger handful would add 50–100 kcal. Rice 200/4g/0.6g and rasam 40/2g/1g at established rates. **Realistic range on the day: ~2,700–2,950 kcal.**

This exact combination has now been flagged on **Aug 25, Aug 27, Aug 28 and Aug 31**.

🔴 **Fiber ~33.8g before dinner exists — already ~4–9g past the whole day's 25–30g target.** Breakfast spent 18.6g, lunch added 15.2g. **Dinner now has to carry near-zero fiber just to stop the overshoot getting worse**, which is the same corner this log has painted itself into on every weekday since Aug 24. ⚠️ **Every weekday since Aug 24 has closed over the fiber target** (32.4 · 39.2 · 37.1 · 39.6 · 35.9), and today is on the same path.

⚠️ **What would have fixed it, stated with the arithmetic, since the general version clearly isn't landing:** half a red banana (−1.5g) + 1 tbsp PB (−1g) = **−2.5g fiber and −190 kcal**, closing lunch at ~12.7g and the day at ~31.3g before dinner. **Still over.** 🔴 **That is the finding worth taking from today: the lunch fix alone is no longer sufficient, because breakfast came in at 18.6g.** On a 5-fruit breakfast morning, lunch cannot rescue the day — **the two have to be managed together, and breakfast is the bigger lever.**

✅ **Calories ~2,005 with both teas in — on a good line.** ~295–395 kcal available for dinner to land inside 2,300–2,400. **That is a normal light dinner, unlike yesterday**, when lunch left only ~205–305 and dinner came in at 800. **Today the band is comfortably reachable — the failure mode is repeating last night, not the arithmetic.**

✅✅ **Protein ~97.2g — only ~2.8g needed from dinner to clear the 100g floor.** A single egg clears it with room to spare. **This is the least constrained the protein/calorie trade-off has been all week**, and the contrast with yesterday is the point: on Aug 30 the floor needed 22.8g out of ≤305 kcal, which forced a specific dinner; today essentially any dinner inside the calorie room works.

🔴 **Newly relevant after the 2026-08-31 target decision: the gain rate is running ~1.71 kg/month against a revised 0.5–0.9 target** ([[bmi-increase-plan]]). **Landing inside 2,300–2,400 today matters more than it did last week** — the band was never actually tested, and Aug 24–30 averaged ~2,572/day. **A ~2,350 close today is the first data point of that test.**

**Dinner notes — 2026-08-31:**

✅ **Dinner ~345 kcal / 19.5g protein / 4.5g fiber — right shape, but the margin narrowed twice after the fact.** Eggs, no chicken/fish, light ([[profile]] dinner rules ✅). Against last night's 800 kcal dinner it is still **well under half**, and it is the first dinner this week that responded to the mid-day arithmetic instead of repeating lunch. ⚠️ It landed at the **top of the ~295–395 kcal room** rather than mid-range, because two items were added after the first close: sambar (+70) and fried rather than boiled eggs (+45).

✅✅ **Calories closed at ~2,350 — INSIDE the 2,300–2,400 band, and this time mid-band rather than scraping the floor.** Of Aug 26–30 only Aug 28's 2,335 landed in the band (3,089 · 2,425 · 2,335 · 2,520 · 2,895) and the week averaged ~2,572. **Today's stated test — "can the band actually be hit" — passed cleanly.** ⚠️ **Note how it got there: as first logged the day read 2,235 (under the floor); sambar and the fried-egg correction added 115 kcal between them.** The band was hit, but the margin came from items that surfaced only on questioning.

🔴🔴 **But hitting the band immediately exposed a contradiction between the band and the gain target, and this is the most important finding of the day.** ~2,350 against ~2,050 maintenance = **~300 kcal/day surplus ≈ 1.17 kg/month** — **well above** the revised **0.5–0.9 kg/month** target set this morning in [[bmi-increase-plan]], and ~30% over its ceiling. Run it backwards: 0.5–0.9 kg/month = **128–231 kcal/day surplus = an intake band of ~2,180–2,280 kcal/day.** 🔴 **The current 2,300–2,400 band was set when the gain target was 0.8–1.0 kg/month. It was never recomputed when the target was revised down. As it stands, perfectly hitting the *bottom* of the calorie band overshoots the *top* of the rate target.**

⚠️ **This revives the Aug 17 trim question on completely different grounds — and this time the argument holds.** This morning's weight-table note rejected the trim because "the lever is adherence, not a lower number," which was correct against an intake of ~2,572 that was ignoring the ceiling anyway. **Today adherence happened, and the moment it did, the band's own arithmetic failed.** That is a different objection and it is not answered by adherence. ⚠️ **Caveat before acting: ~2,050 maintenance is an estimate carried unchanged since July at 58 kg, and maintenance rises with bodyweight — at 61.2 kg it is plausibly ~2,100–2,150, which would shift the recomputed band up by ~50–100 kcal.** **Recommend recomputing maintenance and the band together rather than trimming on the old figure. Not changed in [[bmi-increase-plan]] — flagged for decision.**

✅✅ **Protein ~116.7g — mid-band and the cleanest protein close of the week.** ⚠️ **Conditional on the Buddha bowl, and that condition is now load-bearing: 7th consecutive lunch logged at the non-veg 45g default (Aug 19, 20, 21, 25, 27, 28, 31).** If it was vegetarian, today closes at **~96.7g — under the 100g floor**, and so do several prior days. **This single unconfirmed item is the largest source of error in the log and it is now a week old.**

🔴 **Fiber ~38.3g — ~8g over target, 8th consecutive weekday over.** Dinner contributed 4.5g, so **the overshoot was locked in before dinner for the eighth time running.** This confirms the finding written at lunch: with an 18.6g breakfast, **no dinner can rescue the day — breakfast is the lever, not lunch and not dinner.**

✅ **First day in three with no chicken 65** (14 pieces across Aug 29–30). The histamine load is finally unloaded, which means **tomorrow morning is the first clean airway data point since Aug 28** — the five airway screening questions open since Aug 19 are worth answering on it.

⚠️ **Idli is fermented — mild histamine/sinus relevance**, same minor note as Jul 20 and Aug 20/25. Not a problem at 2 pieces; listed only for consistency.

⚠️ **Restaurant style + sambar both confirmed after the day was first closed; the entry above is the corrected version.** As first logged it read "2 idli, 2 eggs" with no sambar and closed at **~2,235 / ~113.2g / ~35.8g — 65 kcal under the band floor.** Sambar was queried because **every prior restaurant idli entry in this log came with it** (Jul 20, Jul 25, Aug 20, Aug 25) and it had never once appeared standalone; it was confirmed and added at the established small-cup rate of 70 / 3g / 2g. 🔴 **The 70 kcal moved the day from "missed the band low" to "inside the band" — worth recording as a logging lesson: on the one day set up as an explicit test of the calorie band, an unreported 70 kcal side dish inverted the result.** **Report sides with restaurant meals; they are not rounding errors here.** ⚠️ Idli style itself changed nothing — the log rates restaurant and home style identically at 45 / 2g / 1g per piece.

⚠️ **Eggs were also corrected after the close: not boiled, but 1 onion omelette + 1 half-fry.** Rated at the log's established fried-egg figures — **1 egg omelette 90 / 6g / 0g** (Jul 24) with **+5 kcal / +0.5g fiber for onion** (cf. the Jul 22 capsicum-onion omelette at 100/6.5g per egg), and the half-fry at the same 90 / 6g / 0g since it is the same egg in the same pan of oil. **Boiled would have been 140 / 12g / 0g; fried is 185 / 12.5g / 0.5g — a +45 kcal difference that is almost entirely absorbed cooking oil.** 🔴 **Third consecutive day carrying added frying oil** — Aug 29 chicken 65 (10 pc), Aug 30 ladies finger twice + banana chips (~340 kcal of it), Aug 31 two fried eggs. **Against HDL 32** ([[lab-reports-summary]]) the relevant number is not any single day but the run. ⚠️ **Two after-the-fact corrections in one entry (~115 kcal, ~5% of the day) is itself the finding:** the difference between "boiled" and "fried" and the presence of a side dish are exactly the details that decide whether a day lands in band, and both had to be asked for.


⚠️ **"Buddha bowl" veg/non-veg still unconfirmed — 7th consecutive lunch entry** (Aug 19, 20, 21, 25, 27, 28, 31) valued at the non-veg default of 595/45g/10g. **If it is vegetarian, protein is overstated by ~20g on all seven days**, which would move several of those closings from "inside the 100–120g band" to below the floor. This is the largest single uncertainty in the log.

**Note:** Values at established rates — milkshake 665/23.2g/10.4g, 2 boiled eggs 140/12g/0g, pomegranate 100/1.6g/4g (doubled from the established ½ pomegranate 50/0.8g/2g, Jul 25), ½ mosambi 30/0.5g/1g, ½ guava 30/1g/2.5g (Jul 20), grapes ~35/0.4g/0.5g (half the established ~100g handful rate of 70/0.7g, Aug 1). **1 strawberry is a first appearance and is nutritionally negligible (~5 kcal)** — logged for completeness, not because it moves any number.

**Notes — 2026-09-02 (running; day open):**

🔴🔴🔴 **Breakfast fiber ~25.9g — that is the ENTIRE daily 25–30g target, eaten before 8 AM.** Previous record was **21.8g yesterday**, which this log already called the highest breakfast ever recorded. **This beats it by 4.1g.** ⚠️ **Yesterday's written conclusion, one day old, was: *"the fruit stack has to come down to pay for [the sunflower seeds and dates]. Dropping the pomegranate and custard apple from a Sep 1-style breakfast would put it at ~16.9g."* The fruit stack went up instead.**

⚠️ **Attribution, stated precisely, because the recipe change is the obvious suspect and it is not the main one.** The +4.1g over yesterday breaks down as: **padam pisin +4.0** · milkshake recipe **+1.5** · papaya **+1.5** · pancake **+1.0** · mosambi **−1.0** (half, not whole) · custard apple **−2.4** · chicken soup **−0.5**. 🔴 **Padam pisin alone is the single largest contributor — more than the recipe change.** The new milkshake accounts for ~37% of the increase; the food choices account for the rest.

🔴🔴 **The day is now arithmetically finished on fiber before lunch is eaten.** 25.9g spent against a 25–30g target leaves **0–4.1g for lunch and dinner combined.** A Buddha bowl alone is ~10g. **A usual Buddha-bowl lunch (15.2g with banana + PB) closes the day pre-dinner at ~41.1g — which would be the highest fiber day in the entire log.** **This will be the 10th consecutive weekday over target.**

🔴 **Gastric relevance is not theoretical today, and the timing is bad.** A headache was reported yesterday afternoon and attributed to workload. **This morning's breakfast is a high-fermentation load on top of it:** padam pisin is a **soluble gum** — the most fermentable item in the whole rotation — plus pomegranate, guava, fig and the shake's PB and seeds. [[gastric-remedies]] already flagged this exact combination once (Aug 18: *"this is a high-fermentation breakfast — don't eat it on a gassy morning"*). ⚠️ **If a forehead headache appears 1–2 hours after lunch today, breakfast is a live cause and not only the Buddha bowl.**

✅ **Papaya is the one genuinely good new addition and deserves the credit.** ~40 / 0.5g / 1.5g, first appearance in the log. **Papain is a digestive enzyme and papaya is mildly laxative** — of everything added this morning it is the item best matched to the current gastric thread, and it is the smallest fiber contributor of the four fruits.

✅ **Protein ~44.1g at breakfast — the strongest breakfast protein of the week**, up from 39.7g (Aug 31) and 36.5g (Sep 1). ✅ **Padam pisin is also a coolant and was part of the original [[bmi-increase-plan]] breakfast base** — it is not junk, and this is not an argument for dropping it permanently. **It is an argument that padam pisin and a four-fruit stack cannot both be in the same breakfast.**

🔴 **Calories ~1,276 — the largest breakfast in at least two weeks** (vs 1,040 on Aug 31, 1,095 on Sep 1). Against the 2,300–2,400 band that leaves **~1,024–1,124 kcal for lunch AND dinner combined.** A usual Buddha-bowl lunch is **965**, which would leave **~60–160 kcal for dinner — effectively no dinner.** 🔴 **Against the recomputed ~2,180–2,280 band it is worse: ~904–1,004 for both meals, i.e. the usual lunch alone nearly closes the day.**

⚠️ **Today's lunch therefore has to change, and the arithmetic says how.** **Buddha bowl + buttermilk + tea, with NO banana and NO peanut butter = 665 / 48g / 10.2g.** That closes pre-dinner at **~1,941 / ~92.1g / ~36.1g** and leaves **~359–459 kcal for a normal dinner** — the same room that worked the last two nights. **Dropping the banana and PB is worth 300 kcal and 5g fiber today; on any other day it has been worth arguing about, today it is the only thing that makes dinner possible.**

**Lunch notes — 2026-09-02:**

✅✅ **Banana dropped entirely, PB cut to 1 tbsp — the lowest-fiber lunch configuration recorded in this log, better on fiber than even the Aug 17 version.** *(Reported in two parts; the 1 tbsp PB was added after the bowl/buttermilk/tea line, so this note supersedes an initial reading of the lunch as a full no-PB cut.)* Compare the three versions of the fix:
> | Date | Banana | PB | Add-on fiber | Add-on kcal |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | **Aug 17** | half | none | 1.5g | 55 |
> | **Aug 28** | full | 1 tbsp | 4.0g | 205 |
> | **Sep 2** | **none** | **1 tbsp** | **1.0g** | **95** |
>
> **Today is the lowest fiber of the three.** Against the usual 2-tbsp-PB-plus-full-banana lunch it is worth **~205 kcal and ~4g fiber.** **This combination is the single most-flagged item in the log — Aug 25, 27, 28, 31, Sep 1 — and it has finally moved.**

🔴🔴 **And it still isn't enough — which is the cleanest possible proof of the breakfast finding.** Compare two days where the *identical* lunch fix was applied:
> **Aug 17** — fix applied (1.5g of lunch add-on fiber) → pre-dinner **27.9g**, inside target
> **Sep 2** — *stronger* fix applied (1.0g of lunch add-on fiber) → pre-dinner **37.1g**, ~7g over target
>
> **Today's lunch was 0.5g LOWER in add-on fiber than Aug 17's and the day still came out 9.2g higher. The entire difference is breakfast.** ⚠️ **This retires any remaining version of "fix lunch and the fiber problem goes away." It doesn't — the lunch lever has now been pulled past where it was on the best day in the log, and the day is still over.** Even so: **without the cut, today would sit at ~41.1g pre-dinner, the highest in the log. The fix prevented a record; it could not prevent an overshoot.**

⚠️ **Fiber ~37.1g before dinner — ~7g over, 10th consecutive weekday over target.** Dinner must carry near-zero fiber.

⚠️ **Calories ~2,036 — dinner room ~264–364 kcal against the current band.** Workable, though tighter than the ~359–459 the no-PB version would have allowed. 🔴 Against the recomputed **~2,180–2,280** band it is **~144–244 — roughly two eggs and little else.**

✅ **Protein ~96.1g — and the 1 tbsp of PB is what makes the day rescuable, which is a real change from the no-PB version.** 9th consecutive lunch at the unconfirmed non-veg 45g default. **If the bowl was vegetarian the day sits at ~76.1g, and 4 boiled eggs (280 kcal, 24g) inside the ~364 kcal room lands at ~100.1g — clearing the floor exactly.** Without the PB it would have landed at ~96g and failed. ⚠️ **The margin is now zero, not negative: it works only if dinner is specifically 4 eggs.** Yesterday and today were both unrescuable on the earlier arithmetic; **today the PB bought the rescue back, by 0.1g.**

⚠️ **Dinner brief: ~264–364 kcal, near-zero fiber, ≥4g protein, no chicken/fish, before 6:30 PM.** **1 cup rice + 2 boiled eggs = 340 / 16g / 0.6g** → day closes **~2,376 / ~112.1g / ~37.7g**, in band on all three. Adding rasam (+40) gives 2,416, **16 kcal past the ceiling — noise, fine either way.** 🔴 **The last two nights' 2 idli + 2 eggs (230) now only just works** — it would close at ~2,266, under the old floor but inside the recomputed band. **No sambar, no chutney, no vegetable side.**

**Dinner reported — 3 idli (restaurant style) + 2 boiled eggs.** 135/6g/3g + 140/12g/0g = **275 / 18g / 3g.**

⚠️ **Corrected after the day was first closed: 1½ small cup sambar + 1 small cup coconut chutney were also had, and had not been reported.** As first logged, dinner read 275/18g/3g and the day closed at 2,311/114.1g/40.1g. Rated at established rates — sambar small cup **70/3g/2g** (per Aug 31/Sep 1 precedent), scaled ×1.5 for the half-cup extra → **105/4.5g/3g**; coconut chutney small cup ≈ **60/1g/1g** (scaled from the log's 3 tbsp = 60/1g/1g rate). Combined addition: **165 kcal / 5.5g protein / 4g fiber.** This is the same logging pattern flagged on Aug 31 — **every restaurant idli dinner in this log has come with sambar** (Jul 20, Jul 25, Aug 20, Aug 25, Aug 31), and it again surfaced only on follow-up.

🔴 **Calories now close at ~2,476 — ~76–176 kcal past the OLD 2,300–2,400 band's ceiling, and ~196–296 past the recomputed ~2,180–2,280 band.** This is no longer a noise-level miss like the pre-correction 2,311; it's a real overshoot, driven entirely by two unreported side dishes. **Sambar + chutney together (165 kcal) is a bigger single swing than the "3 idli vs 2 idli" difference flagged in the original dinner brief (45 kcal).**

🔴🔴🔴 **Fiber now closes at ~44.1g — the correction alone (4g) is larger than the gap between today's pre-correction total and the previous all-time record (40.1g vs 39.6g, a 0.5g gap).** The day was already the highest fiber on record before this was added; it is now higher still. **10th consecutive weekday over the 25–30g target**, now at 44.1g against it — the largest single-day overshoot in the log (previous largest gap to target was ~14.6g on Aug 27; today's gap is ~14–19g even at the low end of the range). Combined with this morning's high-fermentation breakfast (padam pisin, pomegranate, guava, fig) and yesterday's afternoon headache, tonight is now unambiguously the highest-fermentation-load evening in the log — the thing to watch for tomorrow morning is a forehead headache specifically, per the overnight-fermentation mechanism in [[gastric-remedies]] (distinct from the post-lunch pattern).

✅ **Protein now closes at ~119.6g — still clears the 100–120g floor, now sitting right at the top of it rather than mid-band**, with the extra 5.5g coming for free from the sambar/chutney correction. The zero-margin "day only works if dinner is specifically 4 eggs" rescue scenario flagged at lunch was never actually needed.

⚠️ **Logging lesson, repeated:** this is at least the third time in this log that an unreported restaurant side dish (sambar, chutney, or both) has changed whether a day reads as "in band" — Aug 31 (sambar, +70 kcal, moved the day from under-floor to in-band) and now Sep 2 (sambar + chutney, +165 kcal, moved the day from marginal-over to clearly-over). **Sides are not rounding errors in this log; ask about them every time a restaurant/tiffin-style meal is reported standalone.**

A milk-only dinner was the right call for the numbers as first calculated and the wrong one for the numbers as corrected.

✅🔴 **Fiber ~21.8g — the 11-consecutive-weekday over-target streak is broken, but by undershooting, not by landing inside.** Target is 25–30g; the day finished **~3.2g short on the low side**, the first weekday under target since ~Aug 20. **The lunch note called this exactly** — *"if dinner adds a fruit or vegetable, this could be the first day in 11 to land inside the target"* — and a zero-fiber dinner sent it under instead. **1 pomegranate (100 / 1.6g / 4g) would have closed at 25.8g, inside the band for the first time in nearly two weeks.** Direction is right after the 44.1g record three days ago (44.1 → 33.1 → 21.8); the overshoot problem is genuinely resolving, but the correction has now gone one step past the target rather than stopping in it.

⚠️ **Calories ~2,251 — below the old 2,300 floor by ~49 kcal, but inside the recomputed ~2,180–2,280 band.** 🔴 **This is the first day in this log where the two calorie bands return opposite verdicts.** Aug 31 through Sep 3 all read "in the old band, over the recomputed one" — the disagreement always ran the same direction and never changed whether the day was judged a hit. **Today it inverts: old band says the day is short, recomputed band says it is a clean mid-range close.** ⚠️ **The band decision flagged on Aug 31 and unresolved in [[bmi-increase-plan]] for five days has stopped being academic — it now determines whether this day is logged as a miss or a hit.** (Only prior day inside the recomputed band was Aug 24 at 2,252 — near-identical to today.)

⚠️ **A glass of milk is not a dinner, and both readings of that are worth stating.** Against [[profile]]'s *"light days below 2,300 push every [[bmi-increase-plan]] milestone later"* at BMI ~18.0, a 130 kcal dinner is a skipped meal in a weight-gain phase. **But the counterargument is real:** lunch was a heavy beef-and-fat meal (~1,095 kcal), and a small, low-volume, fiber-free dinner is a defensible gastric call on top of it — it is the opposite of the slow-emptying profile [[gastric-remedies]] links to next-morning headache. **The cost was not the lightness; it was the composition.** 2 eggs and a pomegranate (240 kcal) would have been almost as gentle and would have cleared all three targets.

**Rates used:** rice 300/6g/0.9g (established, 1½ cup) · Biscoff 37/0.3g/0.1g each (set today, see correction above) · tea 35/1g/0g (established). **Two new estimates, both first-time items in this log:**

- **Crab, 1½ big — 150 kcal / 27g protein.** Crab meat is one of the leanest animal proteins there is (~85 kcal and ~18g protein per 100g edible). A large mud/sea crab yields roughly 100g of picked meat, so 1½ ≈ 150g meat. ⚠️ **"Big" is doing a lot of work here** — yield scales with shell size and this could be 100g or 200g of meat; **±60 kcal / ±10g protein.**
- **Crab gravy — 120 kcal / 5g / 1g.** Priced above the established fish-gravy rate (60/2g/0.5g) because Madurai crab masala is typically coconut- and oil-heavier, and **this line is where the cooking oil is booked** — the crab line above is meat only, to avoid double-counting.
- **Frooti small bottle — 150/0g/0g**, assuming the 250ml PET. ⚠️ **If it was the 160ml Tetra Pak, it's ~100 kcal.** Either way it is **~30–37g of pure sugar with no protein, no fiber and no micronutrients.**
- **Banana chips, "few" — 60/0.5g/0.5g**, from the log's established rate of **10 pieces = 110/1g/1g** (set Jul 23, reused Aug 30), read as ~5–6 pieces. ⚠️ **"Few" is soft — a handful of 10 doubles this to 110.**

## Micronutrient & Supplement Tracking (added 2026-08-13)

> **Why this is a tick-box table and not mg columns.** Per-meal micronutrient totals were considered and rejected: (1) estimation error for micronutrients exceeds the signal — magnesium in the same dish varies by soil, variety, and preparation, well beyond the ±15–20% already carried by the calorie estimates; (2) **vitamin D, the one confirmed deficiency in [[lab-reports-summary]], is not food-trackable** — it comes from sunlight and supplement; (3) **folate risk here is drug-induced, not dietary** — sulfasalazine blocks absorption, so intake tells you nothing about status; (4) for micronutrients, intake is a weak proxy for status, unlike the direct calories → weight chain. **Blood levels are the real measure — they live in [[lab-reports-summary]].** This table tracks the two things a food log genuinely can: whether the anchor foods happened, and whether the supplements were taken.

### The food anchors — mostly one item

**The daily milkshake covers both key anchors on its own:** milk ~200ml + 1 red banana + 2 tbsp PB + 6 almonds + 5 cashews + 10 pumpkin seeds + 1 tsp flax + 5 dry grapes + 3 walnut halves + **1 dried fig (anjeer) — added 2026-08-17** ≈ **~186mg magnesium (~47% RDA)** and **~1.1g omega-3 ALA**. So the practical question most days is simply *did the shake happen* — the overnight-oats variant (Aug 11) substitutes for it adequately (oats + chia + flax + walnuts).

> **Standard values updated 2026-08-17: "Milkshake (usual recipe)" = ~665 kcal · 23.2g protein · 10.4g fiber** (was 635 / 22.8g / 9g). Entries before Aug 17 keep the old figures — they did not contain the fig.
>
> **Why the fig earns its place — and what it does not do.**
> ✅ **Calcium is the real reason.** Dried figs are among the best non-dairy calcium sources (~162mg/100g, ~19mg per fig). [[profile]] records serum calcium 8.8 (normal) and this file explicitly rules out calcium *tablets* — but **BMI 17.7 is itself an independent bone-density risk factor**, and bone is [[healthspan-80]] Pillar 3. Dietary calcium from a daily anchor is the right way to add it. Figs also bring potassium and are prebiotic.
> 🔴 **It does nothing for the calorie problem.** ~30 kcal is a rounding error against a weekend that ran ~600–800 kcal short. **If the aim is to make the shake work harder, dates beat figs roughly 2:1** — the Jul 22 variant (shake + 2 dates) added ~130 kcal, and an extra tbsp of PB adds ~95 kcal with 4g protein. A fig is a micronutrient add, not a calorie add; don't count on it for gain.
> ⚠️ **It pushes fiber the wrong way on weekdays.** The shake already carries 9g, and weekday fiber ran 36–42g for five consecutive days against a 25–30g target. Adding 1.4g to a *daily* item makes the weekday overshoot marginally worse while helping the weekend collapse (4.8g on Aug 16). **The fiber problem is a weekday/weekend split, and a daily addition is the one shape that cannot fix it.**

| Anchor | Target | Counts as |
|--------|--------|-----------|
| **Magnesium** (~400mg/day RDA) | daily | Milkshake · OR 40g oats + nuts/seeds · OR a **handful (28g) of pumpkin seeds ≈ 150mg**, the densest single source · OR 20–30g dark chocolate |
| **Omega-3 ALA** | daily | Milkshake (flax + walnuts) · OR walnuts · OR 1 tsp ground flax · OR 1 tsp chia · OR fatty fish |

> ⚠️ **Flaxseed must be GROUND, not whole.** Whole flaxseed passes through undigested — the ALA is not absorbed. If the 1 tsp in the shake is whole seed, that portion of the omega-3 is being wasted. **Confirm which form is being used.**

> 🔴 **Weekend anchor gap, first time visible in this table.** The milkshake carries ~45% of the magnesium RDA and essentially all the dietary omega-3 ALA. It was absent **both** weekend days — the same structural weekend hole that drops fiber to single digits. Magnesium matters here specifically for migraine, sleep onset and muscle ([[bmi-increase-plan]] prescribes 200mg glycinate nightly, adherence still unrecorded), and the disturbed-sleep stretch Aug 11–13 ran straight into this gap. **A weekend fix that solves fiber, magnesium and omega-3 at once: keep the milkshake on Saturday and Sunday, when the Buddha bowl isn't there to cover.**

> 🔴 **All three prescribed supplements have unknown status.** [[bmi-increase-plan]] prescribes D3 60K weekly (Vit D was 29.95 = insufficient), magnesium glycinate 200mg nightly (migraine + sleep + muscle), and omega-3 1g daily (HDL 32). **None has ever been recorded as taken or stopped.** Given vitamin D was last measured **July 2024** — two years stale — adherence here matters more than any food-tracking refinement. Answer these three and the table becomes useful immediately.

### What needs a blood test, not a food log

|--------|--------|----------------------------|
| **Vitamin D** | Last measured **Jul 2024** (29.95, insufficient) — 2 years stale | Only confirmed deficiency on record. Bone density ([[healthspan-80]] Pillar 3), immune/allergic function, cortisol baseline |
| **Folate (B9)** | **Never measured** | **Sulfasalazine is a folate antagonist** — on it since Jul 2023, status listed as "likely stopped, confirm with ortho" in [[profile]]. Long-term use is a documented cause of deficiency. Raise with Dr. Rajapandian. |
| **Zinc** | Never measured | Testosterone (never measured despite PE severity), immune function, tissue repair during weight gain |
| **B12** | Never measured | Meat intake is high so deficiency is unlikely — low priority, but cheap to add |
| **Testosterone + SHBG** | Queued for Oct 2026 | [[anxiety-stress]] recommends pulling this forward given PE severity — if it moves up, add the four above to the same draw |

**Explicitly not needed:** **iron** (Hb 15.6, RBC 5.30, PCV 45.6 all mid-normal — supplementing without deficiency causes constipation, already a risk here) and **calcium tablets** (serum 8.8 normal; dairy intake is real — milkshake, curd, buttermilk, paneer, cheese).

> **Remaining watch item (different from the original):** weekends. Jul 25–26 with no bowl still dropped to ~8g. That is the only structural fiber gap left.

> **Calorie trend checkpoint — RESOLVED 2026-08-03.** Jul 27–31 ran 2,500 · 2,487 · 2,573 · 2,427 · 2,600 (avg ~2,517, ~470/day surplus over revised maintenance ≈1.8 kg/month pace). The rule set then was: if the Aug 3 weigh-in shows gain above ~1.2 kg/month, trim ~150/day off breakfast. **Actual result: 59.1 → 59.75 kg in 7 days ≈ 1.9 kg/month pace — past the trigger, and not a one-week blip** (the prior weekly delta, +0.6kg Jul21→27, is consistent with it — two weeks running at this rate). **Recommendation: trim ~150 kcal/day, starting with the milkshake (where the overshoot actually lives), not dinner.** Not yet actioned — flagging for a decision rather than changing the standing 2,300–2,400 target unilaterally, since that number is load-bearing across [[profile]], [[bmi-increase-plan]], and [[healthspan-80]].
